country_event = { # Setup
    id = 12346
    title = "Trigger a Tsunami"
    #desc = ""
    picture = "commonwealth"
	allow_multiple_instances = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
	}
	
	option = {
        name = "Trigger Tsunami In The Marshal Islands"
		3244 = { add_core = CLN }
		current_weather_event = { add_province_modifier = { name = universal_modifier duration = -1 } } 
		3244 = { add_province_modifier = { name = universal_effector_modifier duration = -1 } } 
		CLN = { country_event = 700 }
		set_global_flag = tsunami_active
		CLN = { set_variable = { which = tsunami_age value = 10 } }
    }
	
	option = {
        name = "Trigger Cyclone in Belgium"
		388 = { add_province_modifier = { name = cyclone duration = -1 } }
		set_global_flag = cyclone_active
		CLN = { set_variable = { which = cyclone_age value = 50 } }
    }
	
	option = {
        name = "Trigger Cyclone on Nolfork Island"
		2508 = { add_province_modifier = { name = cyclone duration = -1 } } # Nolforks Provid?
		set_global_flag = cyclone_active
		CLN = { set_variable = { which = cyclone_age value = 50 } }
    }
	
	option = {
        name = "Trigger Tsunami In The Indian Ocean (Argo Zone)"
		3090 = { add_core = CLN }
		set_global_flag = tsunami_active
    }
	
	option = {
        name = "Trigger Tsunami In Hawaii"
		3150 = { add_core = CLN }
		set_global_flag = tsunami_active
		CLN = { set_variable = { which = tsunami_age value = 10 } }
    }
}


country_event = { # Setup
    id = 12348
    title = "Cyclone Cleanup"
    #desc = ""
    #picture = "commonwealth"
	allow_multiple_instances = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		clr_global_flag = sea_cyclone
		clr_global_flag = landlocked_cyclone
		any_land_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = cyclone
			}

		any_sea_province = { limit = { is_core = CLN province_id = 3582 } any_sea_province = { limit = { OR = { province_id = 2960 province_id = 2965 province_id = 2959 } NOT = { is_core = UT2 is_core = CLN } } add_province_modifier = { name = sea_scope_modifier duration = -1 } } remove_core = CLN add_core = UT2 any_neighbor_province = { add_province_modifier = { name = possible_cyclone duration = -1 } } }
		any_sea_province = { limit = { is_core = CLN province_id = 3583 } any_sea_province = { limit = { OR = { province_id = 2971 province_id = 2964 province_id = 2965 province_id = 2966 } NOT = { is_core = UT2 is_core = CLN } } add_province_modifier = { name = sea_scope_modifier duration = -1 } } remove_core = CLN add_core = UT2 any_neighbor_province = { add_province_modifier = { name = possible_cyclone duration = -1 } } }
		any_sea_province = { limit = { is_core = CLN province_id = 3630 } any_sea_province = { limit = { OR = { province_id = 2779 } NOT = { is_core = UT2 is_core = CLN } } add_province_modifier = { name = sea_scope_modifier duration = -1 } } remove_core = CLN add_core = UT2 any_neighbor_province = { add_province_modifier = { name = possible_cyclone duration = -1 } } }
		any_sea_province = { limit = { is_core = CLN province_id = 3643 } any_sea_province = { limit = { OR = { province_id = 2988 } NOT = { is_core = UT2 is_core = CLN } } add_province_modifier = { name = sea_scope_modifier duration = -1 } } remove_core = CLN add_core = UT2 any_neighbor_province = { add_province_modifier = { name = possible_cyclone duration = -1 } } }
		any_sea_province = { limit = { has_province_modifier = sea_scope_modifier } add_core = CLN remove_province_modifier = sea_scope_modifier }
		any_land_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = cyclone
				is_coastal = no
			}
			set_global_flag = landlocked_cyclone
		}
		any_sea_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = CLN
			}
			set_global_flag = sea_cyclone
		}
	}

	option = {
        name = "Travels On Land"
		# Tsunami Logic
		any_land_province = {
			limit = { 
				has_province_modifier = cyclone
			}
			add_province_modifier = { name = recent_cyclone duration = 2 }
			remove_province_modifier = cyclone
			random_neighbor_province = {
				limit = { 
					NOT = {
						has_province_modifier = recent_cyclone
					}
				}
				add_province_modifier = { name = possible_cyclone duration = -1 }
			}
		}
		any_land_province = {
			limit = { 
				has_province_modifier = possible_cyclone
			}
			remove_province_modifier = possible_cyclone
			add_province_modifier = { name = cyclone duration = -1 }
		}
		ai_chance = { 
			factor = 0.5
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_global_flag = cyclone_sea }
		}
    }
	
	option = {
        name = "Travels Into the Ocean"
		# Tsunami Logic
		any_land_province = {
			limit = { 
				has_province_modifier = cyclone
			}
			add_province_modifier = { name = recent_cyclone duration = 2 }
			remove_province_modifier = cyclone
			sea_zone = {
				add_core = CLN
			}
		}
		clr_global_flag = cyclone_land
		set_global_flag = cyclone_sea
		ai_chance = { 
			factor = 0.5
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_global_flag = cyclone_sea  }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_global_flag = landlocked_cyclone  }
		}
    }
	
	option = {
        name = "Travels Onto Land"
		random_country = {
            limit = { any_owned_province = { has_province_modifier = possible_cyclone } }
            random_owned = {
                limit = { 
                    has_province_modifier = possible_cyclone
                    NOT = { has_province_modifier = recent_cyclone }
                }
                add_province_modifier = { name = cyclone duration = -1 }
				CLN = { country_event = 12353 }
            }
        }
		any_land_province = {
			remove_province_modifier = possible_cyclone
		}
		any_sea_province = {
			remove_core = UT2
			remove_core = CLN
		}
		clr_global_flag = cyclone_sea
		set_global_flag = cyclone_land
		ai_chance = { 
			factor = 0.5
			modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { has_global_flag = cyclone_sea } }
		}
    }
	
	option = {
        name = "Pauses"
		# Tsunami Logic
		ai_chance = { 
			factor = 0.5
		}
    }
}

country_event = { # Setup
    id = 12350
    title = "Wave Riders"
    #desc = ""
    picture = "tsunami"
	allow_multiple_instances = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		tsunami_loc_1 = {}
		slot_1 = {}
		tsunami_loc_2 = {}
	}
	
	option = {
        name = "Nature has rewritten the rules of engagement."
    }
}

country_event = { # Setup
    id = 12351
    title = "Tsunami Coast Setup"
    #desc = ""
    picture = "tsunami"
	allow_multiple_instances = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		any_country = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = tsunami_impact_happened }
				any_owned_province = { has_province_modifier = tsunami }
			}
            random_owned = {
                limit = { 
                    has_province_modifier = tsunami
					owner = { NOT = { has_country_flag = tsunami_impact_happened } }
                }
                owner = { set_country_flag = tsunami_impact_happened }
				owner = { set_country_flag = viewer }
				add_province_modifier = { name = slot_1_province duration = -1 } 
				CLN = { country_event = 201 }
				owner = { clr_country_flag = viewer }
				owner = { country_event = 12352 }
            }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
        name = "Nature has rewritten the rules of engagement."
    }
}

		tsunami_loc_4 = {}
		slot_1 = {}
		tsunami_loc_5 = {}
	}
	
	option = {
        name = "Nature's fury knows no bounds..."
    }
}

country_event = { # Setup
    id = 12353
    title = "Cyclone Coast Setup"
    #desc = ""
    picture = "tsunami"
	allow_multiple_instances = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		any_country = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = cyclone_impact_happened }
				any_owned_province = { has_province_modifier = cyclone }
			}
            random_owned = {
                limit = { 
                    has_province_modifier = cyclone
					owner = { NOT = { has_country_flag = cyclone_impact_happened } }
                }
                owner = { set_country_flag = cyclone_impact_happened }
				owner = { set_country_flag = viewer }
				add_province_modifier = { name = slot_1_province duration = -1 } 
				CLN = { country_event = 201 }
				owner = { clr_country_flag = viewer }
				owner = { country_event = 12354 }
            }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
        name = "Nature has rewritten the rules of engagement."
    }
}
